BARAMATI MUNICIPAL COUNCIL, BARAMATI
Maharashtra Municipal Council, Nagar Panchayats and Industrial Townships Act, 1965
NOTIFICATION
The Maharashtra Municipal Councils, Nagarpanchayats and Industrial Townships Act, (Mah. Act No.
XL of 1965) (hereinafter referred to as the "said Act") after carefully taking into consideration the
objections and suggestions received, in response to the Draft Notification Maharashtra Government
Gazette, Extraordinary Part I-A--Pune Divisional Suppliment, Extraordinary No. 08, dated 18th August
2025 fixing for the Baramati Municipal Council. The number and the extent of the Wards into which the
said Municipal Area shall be divided is shown in the schedule to said Draft Notification. The State

Government or its authorised officer, with the approval of the State Election Commission, Maharashtra
or its authorised officer, hereby makes the following Notification.
Whereas, the State Government or its authorised officer with the approval of the State Election
Commission, Maharashtra or its authorised officer, as specified for Baramati Municipal Council,
Nagarpanchayat under Section 9 and 10 of the Maharashtra Municipal Councils, Nagarpanchayats and
Industrial Townships Act, 1965 the Number of Councillors to be elected and number of Wards is as
follows:-
1. Total number of Councillors - 41
2. Two Councillors Wards - 19
3. Three Councillors Wards - 1
4. Total wards - 20
Now therefore, in exercise of the powers under Section 9, 10 and 341 B of the The Maharashtra
Municipal Councils, Nagarpanchayats and Industrial Townships Act, 1965. The State Government or
its authorised officer with the approval of the State Election Commission, Maharashtra or its authorised
officer hereby fix the number and extent of the Wards into which the said Municipal Council, Nagar
Panchayat area shall be divided as shown in the schedule to this Notification.
This Notification shall take effect for the purpose of the next General Election held immediately
following the date of this Notification.

LONAVALA MUNICIPAL COUNCIL, LONAVALA
Maharashtra Municipal Council, Nagar Panchayats and Industrial Townships Act, 1965
NOTIFICATION
No. LMC1/G.E./final/684/2025.--In exercise of the powers conferred by Section 9, 10 and 341-B of
The Maharashtra Municipal Councils, Nagarpanchayats and Industrial Townships Act, (Mah. Act No.
XL of 1965) (hereinafter referred to as the "said Act") after carefully taking into consideration the
objections and suggestions received, in response to the Draft Notification Maharashtra Government
Gazette, Extraordinary Part I-A--Pune Divisional Suppliment, Extraordinary No. 08, dated 18th August
2025 fixing for the Lonavala Municipal Council. The number and the extent of the Wards into which the
said Municipal Area shall be divided is shown in the schedule to said Draft Notification. The State

Government or its authorised officer, with the approval of the State Election Commission, Maharashtra
or its authorised officer, hereby makes the following Notification.
Whereas, the State Government or its authorised officer with the approval of the State Election
Commission, Maharashtra or its authorised officer, as specified for Lonavala Municipal Council,
Nagarpanchayat under Section 9 and 10 of the Maharashtra Municipal Councils, Nagarpanchayats and
Industrial Townships Act, 1965 the Number of Councillors to be elected and number of Wards is as
follows :--
1. Total number of Councillors - 27
2. Two Councillors Wards - 12
3. Three Councillors Wards - 01
4. Total wards - 13
Now therefore, in exercise of the powers under Section 9, 10 and 341 B of the The Maharashtra
Municipal Councils, Nagarpanchayats and Industrial Townships Act, 1965. The State Government or
its authorised officer with the approval of the State Election Commission, Maharashtra or its authorised
officer hereby fix the number and extent of the Wards into which the said Municipal Council, Nagar
Panchayat area shall be divided as shown in the schedule to this Notification.
This Notification shall take effect for the purpose of the next General Election held immediately
following the date of this Notification.

SASWAD MUNICIPAL COUNCIL, SASWAD
Maharashtra Municipal Councils, Nagarpanchayats and Industrial Townships Act, 1965
NOTIFICATION
No. SMC/2812/2025.--In exercise of the powers conferred by Section 9, 10 and 341-B of The
Maharashtra Municipal Councils, Nagarpanchayats and Industrial Townships Act, (Mah. Act No. XL of
1965) (hereinafter referred to as the "said Act") after carefully taking into consideration the objections
and suggestions received, in response to the Draft Notification Maharashtra Government Gazette,
Extraordinary Part I-A--Pune Divisional Suppliment, Extraordinary No. 08, dated 18th August 2025
fixing for the Saswad Municipal Council. The number and the extent of the Wards into which the said
Municipal Area shall be divided is shown in the schedule to said Draft Notification. The State
Government or its authorised officer, with the approval of the State Election Commission, Maharashtra
or its authorised officer, hereby makes the following Notification.
Whereas, the State Government or its authorised officer with the approval of the State Election
Commission, Maharashtra or its authorised officer, as specified for Saswad Municipal Council,
Nagarpanchayat under Section 9 and 10 of the Maharashtra Municipal Councils, Nagarpanchayats and
Industrial Townships Act, 1965 the Number of Councillors to be elected and number of Wards is as
follows :--
1. Total number of Councillors - 22
2. Two Councillors Wards - 11
3. Three Councillors Wards - Nil
4. Total wards - 11
Now therefore, in exercise of the powers under Section 9, 10 and 341 B of the The Maharashtra
Municipal Councils, Nagarpanchayats and Industrial Townships Act, 1965. The State Government or
its authorised officer with the approval of the State Election Commission, Maharashtra or its authorised
officer hereby fix the number and extent of the Wards into which the said Municipal Council, Nagar
Panchayat area shall be divided as shown in the schedule to this Notification.
This Notification shall take effect for the purpose of the next General Election held immediately
following the date of this Notification.

MALEGAON BK. NAGARPANCHAYAT, MALEGAON BK.
Maharashtra Municipal Councils, Nagar Panchayats and Industrial Townships Act, 1965
NOTIFICATION
Maharashtra Municipal Councils, Nagarpanchayats and Industrial Townships Act, (Mah. Act No. XL of
1965) (hereinafter referred to as the "said Act") after carefully taking into consideration the objections
and suggestions received, in response to the Draft Notification Maharashtra Government Gazette,
Extraordinary Part I-A--Pune Divisional Suppliment, Extraordinary No. 08, dated 18th August 2025
fixing for the Malegaon Bk. Nagarpanchayat. The number and the extent of the Wards into which the
said Municipal Area shall be divided is shown in the schedule to said Draft Notification. The State
Government or its authorised officer, with the approval of the State Election Commission, Maharashtra
or its authorised officer, hereby makes the following Notification.
Whereas, the State Government or its authorised officer with the approval of the State Election
Commission, Maharashtra or its authorised officer, as specified for Malegaon Bk. Nagarpanchayat,
under Section 9 and 10 of the Maharashtra Municipal Councils, Nagarpanchayats and Industrial
Townships Act, 1965 the Number of Councillors to be elected and number of Wards is as follows :--
1. Total number of Councillors - 17
2. Total wards - 17
Now therefore, in exercise of the powers under Section 9, 10 and 341 B of the The Maharashtra
Municipal Councils, Nagarpanchayats and Industrial Townships Act, 1965. The State Government or
its authorised officer with the approval of the State Election Commission, Maharashtra or its authorised
officer hereby fix the number and extent of the Wards into which the said Municipal Council, Nagar
Panchayat area shall be divided as shown in the schedule to this Notification.
This Notification shall take effect for the purpose of the next General Election held immediately
following the date of this Notification.

BARSHI MUNICIPAL COUNCIL, BARSHI
Maharashtra Municipal Council, Nagar Panchayat and Industrial Township Act, 1965
NOTIFICATION
No. GA/Election-4921/2025.--In exercise of the powers conferred by Section 9, 10 and 341-B of
the Maharashtra Municipal Council, Nagarpanchayat and Industrial Townships Act (Mah. Act. No. XL
of 1965) (hereinafter referred to as the “said Act.”) after carefully taking into consideration the
objections and suggestions received, in response to the Draft Notification No. Maharashtra
Government Gazzette, Extraordinary Part One-A-Pune Divisional Supplement, Extraordinary No. 08,
dated Monday 18th August 2025 fixing for the Barshi Municipal Council. The number and the extent of
the Wards into which the said Municipal Area shall be divided is shown in the schedule to said Draft
Notification. The State Government or its authorised officer, with the approval of the State Election
Commission, Maharashtra or its authorised officer, hereby makes the following Notification.
Whereas State Government or its authorised officer with the approval of the State Election
Commission, Maharashtra or its authorised officer, as specified for Barshi Municipal Council under
Section 9,10 of the The Maharashtra Municipal Council, Nagarpanchayt and Industrial Townships Act,
1965 the Number of Councillors to be elected and number of Wards as follows.
Total number of Councilors - 42
Two Councillors wards - 21
Three Councillors Wards - Nill
Total Wards - 21
Now, therefore, in exercise of the powers under Section 9, 10 and 341-B of the The
Maharashtra Municipal Council, Nagarpanchayat and Industrial Township Act, 1965. The State
Government or its authorised officer with the Approval of the State Election Commission, Maharashtra
or its authorised officer hereby fix the number and extent of the Wards into which the said Municipal
Council, Nagarpanchayat area shall be divided as shown in the schedule to this Notification.
This Notification shall take effect for the purpose of the next General Election held immediately
following the date of this Notification.

PANDHARPUR MUNICIPAL COUNCIL, PANDHARPUR
Maharashtra Municipal Council, Nagar Panchayat and Industrial Township Act, 1965
NOTIFICATION
No. MCA/MC-4/KAVI/456/2025.--In exercise of the powers conferred by Section 9, 10 and 341-
B of the Maharashtra Municipal Council, Nagarpanchayat and Industrial Townships Act (Mah. Act. No.
XL of 1965) (hereinafter referred to as the “said Act.”) after carefully taking into consideration the
objections and suggestions received, in response to the Draft Notification No. Maharashtra
Government Gazzette, Extraordinary Part One-A-Pune Divisional Supplement, Extraordinary No. 08,
dated Monday 18th August 2025 fixing for the Pandharpur Municipal Council. The number and the
extent of the Wards into which the said Municipal Area shall be divided is shown in the schedule to
said Draft Notification. The State Government or its authorised officer, with the approval of the State
Election Commission, Maharashtra or its authorised officer, hereby makes the following Notification.
Whereas State Government or its authorised officer with the approval of the State Election
Commission, Maharashtra or its authorised officer, as specified for Pandharpur Municipal Council
under Section 9,10 of the The Maharashtra Municipal Councils, Nagarpanchayats and Industrial
Townships Act, 1965 the Number of Councillors to be elected and number of Wards as follows.
Total number of Councilors - 36
Two Councillors wards - 18
Three Councillors Wards - Nill
Total Wards - 18
Now, therefore, in exercise of the powers under Section 9, 10 and 341-B of the The
Maharashtra Municipal Council, Nagarpanchayat and Industrial Township Act, 1965. The State
Government or its authorised officer with the Approval of the State Election Commission, Maharashtra
or its authorised officer hereby fix the number and extent of the Wards into which the said Municipal
Council, Nagarpanchayat area shall be divided as shown in the schedule to this Notification.
This Notification shall take effect for the purpose of the next General Election held immediately
following the date of this Notification.

KURDUWADI MUNICIPAL COUNCIL, KURDUWADI
Maharashtra Municipal Council, Nagar Panchayat and Industrial Township Act, 1965
NOTIFICATION
No. Election-1602/2025.--In exercise of the powers conferred by Section 9, 10 and 341-B of the
Maharashtra Municipal Council, Nagarpanchayat and Industrial Townships Act (Mah. Act. No. XL of
1965) (hereinafter referred to as the “said Act.”) after carefully taking into consideration the objections
and suggestions received, in response to the Draft Notification No. Maharashtra Government Gazzette,
Extraordinary Part One-A-Pune Divisional Supplement, Extraordinary No. 08, dated Monday,
18th August 2025 fixing for the Kurduwadi Municipal Council. The number and the extent of the Wards
into which the said Municipal Area shall be divided is shown in the schedule to said Draft Notification.
The State Government or its authorised officer, with the approval of the State Election Commission,
Maharashtra or its authorised officer, hereby makes the following Notification.
Whereas State Government or its authorised officer with the approval of the State Election
Commission, Maharashtra or its authorised officer, as specified for Kurduwadi Municipal Council under
Section 9,10 of the The Maharashtra Municipal Council, Nagarpanchayt and Industrial Townships Act,
1965 the Number of Councillors to be elected and number of wards as follows.
Total number of Councilors - 20
Two Councillors wards - 10
Total Wards - 10
Now, therefore, in exercise of the powers under Section 9, 10 and 341-B of the The
Maharashtra Municipal Council, Nagarpanchayat and Industrial Township Act, 1965. The State
Government or its authorised officer with the Approval of the State Election Commission, Maharashtra
or its authorised officer hereby fix the number and extent of the Wards into which the said Municipal
Council, Nagarpanchayat area shall be divided as shown in the schedule to this Notification.
This Notification shall take effect for the purpose of the next General Election held immediately
following the date of this Notification.

MOHOL MUNICIPAL COUNCIL, MOHOL
Maharashtra Municipal Council, Nagar Panchayat and Industrial Township Act, 1965
NOTIFICATION
No. 11852025.--In exercise of the powers conferred by Section 9, 10 and 341-B
of the Maharashtra Municipal Council, Nagarpanchayat and Industrial Townships Act (Mah. Act. No.
XL of 1965) (hereinafter referred to as the “said Act.”) after carefully taking into consideration the

objections and suggestions received, in response to the Draft Notification No. 8 Maharashtra
Government Gazzette, Extraordinary Part One-A-Pune Divisional Supplement, Extraordinary No. 08,
dated Monday 18th August 2025 fixing for the Mohol Municipal Council. The number and the extent of
the Wards into which the said Municipal Area shall be divided is shown in the schedule to said Draft
Notification. The State Government or its authorised officer, with the approval of the State Election
Commission, Maharashtra or its authorised officer, hereby makes the following Notification.
Whereas State Government or its authorised officer with the approval of the State Election
Commission, Maharashtra or its authorised officer, as specified for Nohol Municipal Council under
Section 9,10 of the The Maharashtra Municipal Council, Nagarpanchayt and Industrial Townships Act,
1965 the Number of Councillors to be elected and number of Wards as follows.
1. Total number of Councilors - 20
2. Two Councillors wards - 10
3. Total Wards - 10
Now, therefore, in exercise of the powers under Section 9, 10 and 341-B of the The
Maharashtra Municipal Council, Nagarpanchayat and Industrial Township Act, 1965. The State
Government or its authorised officer with the Approval of the State Election Commission, Maharashtra
or its authorised officer hereby fix the number and extent of the Wards into which the said Municipal
Council, Nagarpanchayat area shall be divided as shown in the schedule to this Notification.
This Notification shall take effect for the purpose of the next General Election held immediately
following the date of this Notification.

ISLAMPUR MUNICIPAL COUNCIL, ISLAMPUR
Maharashtra Municipal Council, Nagar Panchayat and Industrial Township Act, 1965
NOTIFICATION
No. Navi/Nivadnuk/Karya-5/Kavi-504/2025 In exercise of the powers conferred by section 9, 10
& 341 B of The Maharashtra Municipal Council, Nagar Panchayat and Industrial Township Act, (Mah.
Act No. XL of 1965) (hereinafter referred to as the "said Act) after carefully taking into consideration the
objection & suggestion received, in response to the Draft Notification No. 08 dated 18/08/2025 fixing
for the Islampur Municipal Council. The number and the extent of the wards into which the said
municipal area shall be divided are shown in the schedule to said Draft Notification. The State
Government or its authorized officer, with the Approval of the State Election Commission, Maharashtra
or its authorized officer, hereby makes the following Notification.
Whereas the State Government or its authorized officer with the Approval of the State Election
Commission, Maharashtra or its authorized officer, as specified for Islampur Municipal Council, under
section 9 & 10 of the The Maharashtra Municipal Council, Nagar Panchayat and Industrial Township
Act, 1965 the Number of Councilors to be elected and number of wards is as follows :-
1. Total number of councilors - 30
2. Two councilors wards - 15
3. Three councilors wards - 0
4. Total wards - 15
Now, therefore, in exercise of the powers under section 9, 10 & 341 B of The Maharashtra
Municipal Council, Nagar Panchayat and Industrial Township Act, 1965. The State Government or its
authorized officer with the Approval of the State Election Commission, Maharashtra or its authorized
officer hereby fix the number and extent of the wards into which the said Municipal Council area shall
be divided as shown in the schedule to this Notification.
This Notification shall take effect for the purpose of the next general election held immediately
following the date of this Notification.

TASGAON MUNICIPAL COUNCIL, TASGAON
Maharashtra Municipal Council, Nagar Panchayat and Industrial Township Act, 1965
NOTIFICATION
No.S.G. Letter No. UD/504/Election/2025 In exercise of the powers conferred by section 9, 10 &
341 B of The Maharashtra Municipal Council, Nagar Panchayat and Industrial Township Act, (Mah. Act
No. XL of 1965) (hereinafter referred to as the "said Act") after carefully taking into consideration the
objection & suggestion received, in response to the Draft Notification No. RNI NO.MAHBIL/2012/47976
dated 18 / 08 / 2025 fixing for the Tasgaon Municipal Council. The number and the extent of the wards
into which the said municipal area shall be divided is shown in the schedule to said Draft Notification. The
State Government or its authorised officer, with the Approval of the State Election Commission,
Maharashtra or its authorised officer, hereby makes the following Notification.
Whereas the State Government or its authorised officer with the Approval of the State Election
Commission, Maharashtra or its authorised officer, as specified for Tasgaon Municipal Council under
section 9 & 10 of the The Maharashtra Municipal Council, Nagar Panchayat and Industrial Township Act,
1965 the Number of Councillors to be elected and number of wards is as follows –

1. Total number of councillors :- 24
2. Two councillors wards :- 12
3. Total wards :- 12
Now, therefore, in exercise of the powers under section 9, 10 & 341 B of the The Maharashtra
Municipal Council, Nagar Panchayat and Industrial Township Act, 1965. The State Government or its
authorised officer with the Approval of the State Election Commission, Maharashtra or its authorised
officer hereby fix the number and extent of the wards into which the said Municipal Council area shall be
divided as shown in the schedule to this Notification.
This Notification shall take effect for the purpose of the next general election held immediately
following the date of this Notification.

JATH MUNICIPAL COUNCIL, JATH
Maharashtra Municipal Council, Nagar Panchayat and Industrial Township Act, 1965
NOTIFICATION
No. .. ./504//2025 In exercise of the powers conferred by section 9, 10 & 341
B of The Maharashtra Municipal Council, Nagar Panchayat and Industrial Township Act, (Mah.Act No. XL
of 1965) (hereinafter referred to as the "said Act") after carefully taking into consideration the objection &
suggestion received, in response to the Draft Notification . 08 Dated 18/08/2025 fixing for the
Jath Municipal Council. The number and the extent of the wards into which the said municipal area shall
be divided is shown in the schedule to said Draft Notification The State Government or its authorised
officer, with the Approval of the State Election Commission, Maharashtra or its authorised officer, hereby
makes the following Notification.
Whereas the State Government or its authorised officer with the Approval of the State Election
Commission, Maharashtra or its authorised officer, as specified for Jath Municipal Council under section 9
& 10 of the The Maharashtra Municipal Council, Nagar Panchayat and Industrial Township Act, 1965 the
Number of Councilors to be elected and number of wards is as follows:-
1. Total number of councillors – 23
2. Two councillors wards – 10
3. Three councillors wards – 1
4. Total wards – 11
Now, therefore, in exercise of the powers under section 9, 10 & 341 B of the The Maharashtra
Municipal Council, Nagar Panchayat and Industrial Township Act, 1965, The State Government or its
authorised officer with the Approval of the State Election Commission, Maharashtra or its authorised
officer hereby fix the number and extent of the wards into which the said Municipal Council, Nagar
Panchayat area shall be divided as shown in the schedule to this Notification.
This Notification shall take effect for the purpose of the next general election held immediately
following the date of this Notification.

officer hereby fix the number and extent of the wards into which the said Municipal Council, Nagar
Panchayat area shall be divided as shown in the schedule to this Notification.
This Notification shall take effect for the purpose of the next general election held immediately
following the date of this Notification.

SATARA MUNICIPAL COUNCIL, SATARA
Maharashtra Municipal Council, Nagarpanchayat and Industrial Township Act, 1965
NOTIFICATION
and 341-B of the Maharashtra Municipal Councils, Nagarpanchayats and Industrial Townships Act
(Mah. Act. No. XL of 1965) (hereinafter referred to as the “said Act.”) after carefully taking into
consideration the objections and suggestions received, in response to the Draft Notification No. †.¿ÖÖ.¯Ö¡Ö
Extraordinary Part One-A-Pune Divisional Supplement, Extraordinary No. 08, dated Monday 18th
August 2025 fixing for the Satara Municipal Council. The number and the extent of the Wards into
which the said Municipal Area shall be divided is shown in the schedule to said Draft Notification. The
State Government or its authorised officer, with the approval of the State Election Commission,
Maharashtra or its authorised officer, hereby makes the following Notification.
Whereas State Government or its authorised officer with the approval of the State Election
Commission, Maharashtra or its authorised officer, as specified for Satara Municipal Council
Nagarpanchayat under Section 9,10 of the The Maharashtra Municipal Councils, Nagarpanchayts and
Industrial Townships Act, 1965 the Number of Councillors to be elected and number of Wards as
follows.
1. Total number of Councillors - 50
2. Two Councillors wards - 25
3. Total Wards - 25
Now, therefore, in exercise of the powers under Section 9, 10 and 341-B of the The
Maharashtra Municipal Councils, Nagarpanchayats and Industrial Townships Act, 1965. The State
Government or its authorised officer with the approval of the State Election Commission, Maharashtra
or its authorised officer hereby fix the number and extent of the Wards into which the said Municipal
Councils, Nagarpanchayats area shall be divided as shown in the schedule to this Notification.
This Notification shall take effect for the purpose of the next General Election held immediately
following the date of this Notification.

KARAD MUNICIPAL COUNCIL, KARAD
Maharashtra Municipal Council, Nagarpanchayat and Industrial Township Act, 1965
NOTIFICATION
and 341-B of the Maharashtra Municipal Councils, Nagarpanchayats and Industrial Townships Act
(Mah. Act. No. XL of 1965) (hereinafter referred to as the “said Act.”) after carefully taking into
consideration the objections and suggestions received, in response to the Draft Notification in
Maharashtra Government Gazzette, Extraordinary Part One-A-Pune Divisional Supplement,
Extraordinary No. 08, dated 18th August 2025 fixing for the Karad Municipal Council. The number and
the extent of the Wards into which the said Municipal Area shall be divided is shown in the schedule to
said Draft Notification. The State Government or its authorised officer, with the approval of the State
Election Commission, Maharashtra or its authorised officer, hereby makes the following Notification.
Whereas State Government or its authorised officer with the approval of the State Election
Commission, Maharashtra or its authorised officer, as specified for Karad Municipal Council under
Section 9,10 of the The Maharashtra Municipal Councils, Nagarpanchayts and Industrial Townships
Act, 1965 the Number of Councillors to be elected and number of Wards as follows.

1. Total number of Councillors - 31
2. Two Councillors wards - 14
3. Three Councillors wards - 1
4. Total Wards - 15
Now, therefore, in exercise of the powers under Section 9, 10 and 341-B of the Maharashtra
Municipal Councils, Nagarpanchayats and Industrial Townships Act, 1965. The State Government or
its authorised officer with the approval of the State Election Commission, Maharashtra or its authorised
officer hereby fix the number and extent of the Wards into which the said Municipal Councils,
Nagarpanchayats area shall be divided as shown in the schedule to this Notification.
This Notification shall take effect for the purpose of the next General Election held immediately
following the date of this Notification.

PHALTAN MUNICIPAL COUNCIL, PHALTAN
Maharashtra Municipal Council, Nagarpanchayat and Industrial Township Act, 1965
NOTIFICATION
and 341-B of the Maharashtra Municipal Councils, Nagarpanchayats and Industrial Townships Act
(Mah. Act. No. XL of 1965) (hereinafter referred to as the “said Act.”) after carefully taking into
consideration the objections and suggestions received, in response to the Draft Notification in
Maharashtra Government Gazzette, Extraordinary Part One-A-Pune Divisional Supplement,
Extraordinary No. 08, dated Monday 18th August 2025 fixing for the Phaltan Municipal Council. The
number and the extent of the Wards into which the said Municipal Area shall be divided is shown in the
schedule to said Draft Notification. The State Government or its authorised officer, with the approval of
the State Election Commission, Maharashtra or its authorised officer, hereby makes the following
Notification.
Whereas State Government or its authorised officer with the approval of the State Election
Commission, Maharashtra or its authorised officer, as specified for Phaltan Municipal Council under
Section 9,10 of The Maharashtra Municipal Councils, Nagarpanchayts and Industrial Townships Act,
1965 the Number of Councillors to be elected and number of Wards as follows.

1. Total number of Councillors - 27
2. Two Councillors wards - 12
3. Three Councillors wards - 01
4. Total Wards - 13
Now, therefore, in exercise of the powers under Section 9, 10 and 341-B of the The
Maharashtra Municipal Councils, Nagarpanchayats and Industrial Townships Act, 1965. The State
Government or its authorised officer with the approval of the State Election Commission, Maharashtra
or its authorised officer hereby fix the number and extent of the Wards into which the said Municipal
Councils, Nagarpanchayats area shall be divided as shown in the schedule to this Notification.
This Notification shall take effect for the purpose of the next General Election held immediately
following the date of this Notification.

WAI MUNICIPAL COUNCIL, WAI
Maharashtra Municipal Council, Nagarpanchayat and Industrial Township Act, 1965
NOTIFICATION
and 341-B of the Maharashtra Municipal Councils, Nagarpanchayats and Industrial Townships Act
(Mah. Act. No. XL of 1965) (hereinafter referred to as the “said Act.”) after carefully taking into
consideration the objections and suggestions received, in response to the Draft Notification in
Maharashtra Government Gazzette, Extraordinary Part One-A-Pune Divisional Supplement,
Extraordinary No. 08, dated Monday 18th August 2025 fixing for the Wai Municipal Council. The
number and the extent of the Wards into which the said Municipal Area shall be divided is shown in the
schedule to said Draft Notification. The State Government or its authorised officer, with the approval of
the State Election Commission, Maharashtra or its authorised officer, hereby makes the following
Notification.
Whereas State Government or its authorised officer with the approval of the State Election
Commission, Maharashtra or its authorised officer, as specified for Wai Municipal Council under
Section 9,10 of the The Maharashtra Municipal Councils, Nagarpanchayts and Industrial Townships
Act, 1965 the Number of Councillors to be elected and number of Wards as follows.
1. Total number of Councillors - 23
2. Two Councillors wards - 10
3. Three Councillors wards - 01
4. Total Wards - 11
Now, therefore, in exercise of the powers under Section 9, 10 and 341-B of the The
Maharashtra Municipal Councils, Nagarpanchayats and Industrial Townships Act, 1965. The State
Government or its authorised officer with the approval of the State Election Commission, Maharashtra
or its authorised officer hereby fix the number and extent of the Wards into which the said Municipal
Councils, Nagarpanchayats area shall be divided as shown in the schedule to this Notification.
This Notification shall take effect for the purpose of the next General Election held immediately
following the date of this Notification.

1965) (hereinafter referred to as the “said Act”) after carefully taking into consideration the objection &
suggestion received, in response to the Draft Notification Maharashtra Government Gazette,
Extraordinary Part One-A-Pune Divisional Supplement, Monday, dated 18th August 2025 fixing for the
Vadgaon Municipal Council. The number and the extent of the wards into which the said municipal
area shall be divided is shown in the schedule to said Draft Notification. The State Government or its
authorised officer, with the Approval of the State Election Commission, Maharashtra or its authorised
officer, hereby makes the following Notification.
2. Whereas the State Government or its authorised officer with the Approval of the State Election
Commission, Maharashtra or its authorised officer, as specified for Vadgaon Municipal Council, Nagar
Panchayat under section 9 & 10 of the Maharashtra Municipal Council, Nagar Panchayat and Industrial
Township Act, 1965 the Number of Councilors to be elected and number of wards is as follows -
1. Total number of councillors – 20,
2. Two councillors wards – 10,
3. Three councillors wards – 0,
4. Total wards- 10.
(Strike out which is not applicable)
3. Now, therefore, in exercise of the powers under section 9, 10 & 341 B of the Maharashtra
Municipal Council, Nagar Panchayat and Industrial Township Act, 1965. The State Government or its
authorised officer with the Approval of the State Election Commission, Maharashtra or its authorised
officer hereby fix the number and extent of the wards into which the said Municipal Council Nagar
Panchayat are shall be divided shown in the schedule to this notification
4. This Notification shall take effect for the purpose of the next general election held immediately
following the date of this notification.

HUPARI MUNICIPAL COUNCIL, HUPARI
Maharashtra Municipal Council, Nagar Panchayat and Industrial Township Act, 1965
NOTIFICATION
9, 10 and 341 B of the Maharashtra Municipal Council, Nagar Panchayat and Industrial Township Act,
1965 (Mah.Act No.XL of 1965) (hereinafter referred to as the "said Act") after carefully taking into
consideration the objection & suggestion received, in response to the Draft Notification No.1860/2025-
26 Maharashtra Government Gazette, Extraordinary Part One-A Pune Divisional Supplement, dated
18th August 2025 Fixing for the Hupari Municipal Council. The number and the extent of the wards
into which the said municipal area shall be divided is shown in the schedule to said Draft Notification.
The State Government or its authorised officer, with the Approval of the State Election Commission,
Maharashtra or its authorised officer, hereby makes the following Notification.
Whereas the State Government or its authorised officer with the Approval of the State Election
Commission, Maharashtra or its authorised officer, as specified for Hupari Municipal Council under
section 9,10 & 341 B of the The Maharashtra Municipal Council, Nagar Panchayat and Industrial
Township Act, 1965 the Number of Councillors to be elected and number of wards is as follows :-
1. Total number of councillors - 21
2. Two councillors ward - 09
3. Three councillors wards - 01
4. Total wards-10.
Now, therefore, in exercise of the powers under section 9, 10 and 341 B of the Maharashtra
Municipal Council, Nagar Panchayat and Industrial Township Act 1965.The State Government or its
authorised officer with the Approval of the State Election Commission, Maharashtra or its authorised
officer hereby fix the number and extent of the wards into which the said Municipal Council area shall
be divided as shown in the schedule to this Notification.
This Notification shall take effect for the purpose of the next general election held immediately
following the date of this Notification.

MALKAPUR MUNICIPAL COUNCIL, MALKAPUR
Maharashtra Municipal Council, Nagarpanchayat and Industrial Township Act, 1965
NOTIFICATION
No. M.NP./Election/1042/2025.---- In exercise of the powers conferred by section 9, 10 and 341
B of The Maharashtra Municipal Council, Nagar Panchayat and Industrial Township Act. (Mah Act No.
XL of 1965) (hereinafter referred to as the “said Act”) after carefully taking into consideration the
objection and suggestion received, in response to the Draft Notification. Maharashtra Government
Gazette Extraordinary Part One-A-Pune Divisional Supplement Monday, Dated 18th August 2025
fixing for the Malkapur Municipal Council, Nagar Panchayat. The number and the extent of the wards
into which the said municipal area shall be divided is shown in the schedule to said Draft Notification.
The State Government or its authorised officer, with the Approval of the State Election Commission,
Maharashtra or its authorised officer, hereby makes the following Notification.
Whereas, the State Government or its authorised officer with the Approval of the State Election
Commission, Maharashtra or its authorised officer, as specified for Malkapur Municipal Council, Nagar
Panchayat under section 9 and 10 of the The Maharashtra Municipal Council, Nagar Panchayat and
Industrial Township Act, 1965 the Number of Councilors to be elected and number of wards is as
follows :--

1. Total number of councillors - 20
2. Two councillors wards - 10
3. Three councillors wards - 00
4. Total wards - 10
(Strike out which is not applicable)
Now, therefore, in exercise of the powers under section 9, 10 and 341-B of the The Maharashtra
Municipal Council, Nagar Panchayat and Industrial Township Act, 1965. The State Government or its
authorised officer with the Approval of the State Election Commission, Maharashtra or its authorised
officer hereby fix the number and extent of the wards into which the said Municipal Council, Nagar
Panchayat are shall be divided shown in the schedule to this Notification.
This Notification shall take effect for the purpose of the next general election held immediately
following the date of this Notification.

Maharashtra Municipal Council, Nagar Panchayat and Industrial Township Act. 1965 (Mah. Act. No. XL

objection & suggestion received, in response to the Draft Notification No. dated 18/08/2025 fixing for

Maharashtra Municipal Council, Nagarpanchayat and Industrial Township Act, 1965
NOTIFICATION
No. K.M.C./Election/1343/2025-26.---- In exercise of the powers conferred by section 9, 10 &
341 B of The Maharashtra Municipal Council, Nagar Panchayat and Industrial Township Act, (Mah. Act
No. XL of 1965) (hereinafter referred to as the "said Act") after carefully taking into consideration the
objection and suggestion received, in response to the Draft Notification No. O.N./K.M.C./
Election/965/2025-26, dated 18/08/2025 fixing for the Municipal Council, Nagar-parishad. The number
and the extent of the wards into which the said municipal area shall be divided is shown in the
schedule to said Draft Notification. The State Government or its authorised officer, with the Approval of
the State Election Commission, Maharashtra or its authorised officer, hereby makes the following
Notification.
Whereas the State Government or its authorised officer with the Approval of the State Election
Commission, Maharashtra or its authorised officer, as specified for Kurundwad Municipal Council,
Nagarparishad under section 9 and 10 of the The Maharashtra Municipal Council, Nagarpanchayat
and Industrial Township Act, 1965 the Number of Councilors to be elected and number of wards is as
follows :-

Now, therefore, in exercise of the powers under section 9, 10 and 341-B of the The
Maharashtra Municipal Council, Nagarpanchayat and Industrial Township Act, 1965. The State
Government or its authorised officer with the Approval of the State Election Commission, Maharashtra
or its authorised officer hereby fix the number and extent of the wards into which the said Municipal
Council, Nagarparishad area shall be divided as shown in the schedule to this Notification.
This Notification shall take effect for the purpose of the next general election held immediately
following the date of this Notification.

CHANDGAD NAGARPANCHAYAT, CHANDGAD
Maharashtra Municipal Councils, Nagar Panchayats and Industrial Townships Act, 1965
NOTIFICATION
No. NCP/Election-1334/2025--In exercise of the powers conferred by Section 9, 10 and 341-B of
The Maharashtra Municipal Councils, Nagarpanchayats and Industrial Townships Act, (Mah. Act No.
XL of 1965) (hereinafter referred to as the "said Act") after carefully taking into consideration the
objections and suggestions received, in response to the Draft Notification Maharashtra Government
Gazette, Extraordinary Part I-A--Pune Divisional Suppliment, Extraordinary No. 08, dated 18th August
2025 fixing for the Chandgad Nagarpanchayat. The number and the extent of the Wards into which the
said Municipal Area shall be divided is shown in the schedule to said Draft Notification. The State
Government or its authorised officer, with the approval of the State Election Commission, Maharashtra
or its authorised officer, hereby makes the following Notification.
Whereas, the State Government or its authorised officer with the approval of the State Election
Commission, Maharashtra or its authorised officer, as specified for Chandgad Nagarpanchayat, under
Section 9 and 10 of the the Maharashtra Municipal Councils, Nagarpanchayats and Industrial
Townships Act, 1965 the Number of Councillors to be elected and number of Wards is as follows :--
1. Total number of Councillors - 17
2. One Councillors Ward - 17
3. Total Wards - 17

Now therefore, in exercise of the powers under Section 9, 10 and 341 B of the The Maharashtra
Municipal Councils, Nagarpanchayats and Industrial Townships Act, 1965. The State Government or
its authorised officer with the approval of the State Election Commission, Maharashtra or its authorised
officer hereby fix the number and extent of the Wards into which the said Municipal Council, Nagar
Panchayat area shall be divided as shown in the schedule to this Notification.
This Notification shall take effect for the purpose of the next General Election held immediately
following the date of this Notification.
